<span class="command-tag default">default: layer2</span>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="cfgcmd-heading">
    <div class="cfgcmd">set interfaces bonding &lt;tag&gt; mode</div>
    <div class="command-tags">
      <span class="command-tag single">singlevalue</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</dt>
  

<dd>
<span class="command-tag default">default: 802.3ad</span>

Bonding mode

| Value | Help |
|-------|------|
| `xor-hash` |  Distribute based on MAC address |
| `adaptive-load-balance` |  Load balance: adapts based on transmit and receive plus ARP |
| `transmit-load-balance` |  Load balance: adapts based on transmit load and speed |
| `round-robin` |  Load balance: transmit packets in sequential order |
| `broadcast` |  Fault tolerant: transmits everything on all slave interfaces |
| `active-backup` |  Fault tolerant: only one slave in the bond is active |
| `802.3ad` |  IEEE 802.3ad Dynamic link aggregation |

constraint error message: mode must be 802.3ad, active-backup, broadcast, round-robin, transmit-load-balance, adaptive-load-balance, or xor



</dd>
</dl>

    <div class="command-tags">
      <span class="command-tag multi">multivalue</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</dt>
  

<dd>

Short GI capabilities

| Value | Help |
|-------|------|
| `160` |  Short GI for 160 MHz |
| `80` |  Short GI for 80 MHz |

constraint error message: Invalid value



</dd>
</dl>

<dt>
  <div class="cfgcmd-heading">

</dt>
  

<dd>

Enable receiving PPDU using STBC (Space Time Block Coding)

| Value | Help |

</dl>

Enable VHT TXOP Power Save Mode

Station supports receiving VHT variant HT Control field

<span class="command-tag default">default: 0</span>

| `u32:1-14` |  2.4Ghz (802.11 b/g/n) Channel |

Indicate country in which device is operating

Isolate stations on the AP so they cannot see each other

Maximum number of wireless radio stations. Excess stations will be rejected upon authentication request.

    <div class="cfgcmd">set interfaces wireless &lt;tag&gt; mgmt-frame-protection</div>

</dt>
  

<dd>
<span class="command-tag default">default: disabled</span>

Management Frame Protection (MFP) according to IEEE 802.11w

| Value | Help |
|-------|------|
| `required` |  MFP enforced |
| `optional` |  MFP optional |
| `disabled` |  no MFP |

constraint error message: Invalid value

| `g` |  802.11g - 54 Mbits/sec |
| `b` |  802.11b - 11 Mbits/sec |
| `a` |  802.11a - 54 Mbits/sec |

Select security operation mode

    <div class="cfgcmd">set interfaces wireless &lt;tag&gt; security wep key</div>

  </div>

WEP encryption key

| Value | Help |

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="cfgcmd-heading">
    <div class="cfgcmd">set interfaces wireless &lt;tag&gt; security wpa cipher</div>
    <div class="command-tags">
      <span class="command-tag multi">multivalue</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</dt>
  

<dd>

Cipher suite for WPA unicast packets

| Value | Help |
|-------|------|
| `TKIP` |  Temporal Key Integrity Protocol [IEEE 802.11i/D7.0] |
| `CCMP` |  AES in Counter mode with CBC-MAC [RFC 3610, IEEE 802.11i/D7.0] (supported on all WPA2 APs) |
| `CCMP-256` |  AES in Counter mode with CBC-MAC with 256-bit key |
| `GCMP` |  AES in Galois/counter mode with 128-bit key |
| `GCMP-256` |  AES in Galois/counter mode with 256-bit key |

constraint error message: Invalid cipher selection



</dd>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="cfgcmd-heading">
    <div class="cfgcmd">set interfaces wireless &lt;tag&gt; security wpa group-cipher</div>
    <div class="command-tags">
      <span class="command-tag multi">multivalue</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</dt>
  

<dd>

Cipher suite for WPA multicast and broadcast packets

| Value | Help |
|-------|------|
| `TKIP` |  Temporal Key Integrity Protocol [IEEE 802.11i/D7.0] |
| `CCMP` |  AES in Counter mode with CBC-MAC [RFC 3610, IEEE 802.11i/D7.0] (supported on all WPA2 APs) |
| `CCMP-256` |  AES in Counter mode with CBC-MAC with 256-bit key |
| `GCMP` |  AES in Galois/counter mode with 128-bit key |
| `GCMP-256` |  AES in Galois/counter mode with 256-bit key |

constraint error message: Invalid group cipher selection



</dd>
</dl>

<dt>

| `wpa` |  WPA (IEEE 802.11i/D3.0) |

## Operational Mode Commands

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="opcmd-heading">
    <div class="opcmd">show interfaces bridge brief</div>
  </div>
</dt>

<dd>
  Show summary of the specified bridge interface information
</dd>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="opcmd-heading">
    <div class="opcmd">show interfaces bridge detail</div>
  </div>
</dt>

<dd>
  Show detailed bridge interface information
</dd>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="opcmd-heading">
    <div class="opcmd">show interfaces bridge event-log addr</div>
  </div>
</dt>

<dd>
  Show log for network address events
</dd>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="opcmd-heading">
    <div class="opcmd">show interfaces bridge event-log link</div>
  </div>
</dt>

<dd>
  Show log for network link events
</dd>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="opcmd-heading">
    <div class="opcmd">show interfaces bridge event-log neigh</div>
  </div>
</dt>

<dd>
  Show log for neighbor table events
</dd>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="opcmd-heading">
    <div class="opcmd">show interfaces bridge event-log route</div>
  </div>
</dt>

<dd>
  Show log for route events
</dd>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>
  <div class="opcmd-heading">
    <div class="opcmd">show interfaces bridge event-log rule</div>
  </div>
</dt>

<dd>
  Show log for PBR rule change events
</dd>
</dl>
